# Household hh_s109 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, dining, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Yuki** (resident_1): works outside the home; very tidy (tidiness 0.78); average timing (jitter scale 1.03); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.64); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: friends, photography, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Felix** (resident_2): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.81); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.52); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.69); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the office desk. Hobbies: friends, napping, reading_living. Chores they take on: grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Felix.

## Day 0 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d0`: Yuki hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:11–16:01).
- `ep:deadline:resident_1:d0:looming`: Yuki has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d0`: Felix is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:13–16:27).

Internal states (0–1): Yuki energy 0.67, hurriedness 0.67, distraction 0.21; Felix energy 0.53, hurriedness 0.23, distraction 0.20

## Day 3 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:early_night:resident_2:d3`: Felix turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d3`: Yuki is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (15:47–17:47).
- `running_late:resident_1`: Yuki is running late all day (0.84): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

## Day 6 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d6`: Felix hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:03–17:24).
- `running_late:resident_1`: Yuki is running late all day (0.78): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

## Day 21 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d21`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:24–15:54).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d21`: Felix is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:59–18:39).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_2:d21`: Felix can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (21:10–24:00).
- `unwell_spell:resident_1` (Yuki): Unwell for the day: resting, dosing, drinking more, not working and not going out. no work and no trips out; the day is spent between the bed, the sofa and a chair by the window, and a different thing is left behind in each.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Yuki has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Yuki energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.00, distraction 0.17; Felix energy 0.48, hurriedness 0.35, distraction 0.18

## Day 31 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d31`: Felix hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:24–16:14).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d31`: Yuki is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (14:38–15:08).
